Coverage against the Chai test plan

Observation
-----------
The parent zone record-set count increased by 51, but the Activity Log has zero events for a resource associated with tester, cntrlplane, or 4826. Instead, all 384 parent-zone write/delete events are A, TXT, or CNAME changes made by unrelated CI/external-dns service principals, using names such as *.apps.public-<hash>, *.apps.upgrade-<hash>, *.apps.external-oidc-<hash>, and matching TXT ownership records. The child zone has zero events and unchanged metadata. The shared NS delegation record for tester is present but was not modified during the window.

Conclusion
----------
The parent-zone count delta is consistent with concurrent CI/external-dns reconciliation in the shared hcp-sm-azure zone, not with a change made by this HostedCluster test. The pre-test evidence did not retain a full record-by-record parent-zone listing, so an exact reconstructed name-level pre/post diff is not possible; this limitation is stated in the report. No DNS or Azure resource was modified or deleted during this investigation.

Limitations and honest gaps
    

  • T4/T5 are partial. The existing focused predicate and reconciliation tests pass, but the expanded Chai matrix was not implemented: above-desired counters, nil current-set counters, multiple old sets, observedGeneration > generation, revision-vs-generation cases, list-order permutations, full zero/nil reconciliation fixtures, repeat-reconcile/idempotence, and MachineSet-list error/recovery.
    • 

  • T8 is blocked/not tested. No verified pre-1.14 manager, second CAPI-backed provider, or InPlace smoke environment was available. These rows are not implied by the Azure Replace results.
    • 

  • T1 live watch coverage has two documented gaps because runners were interrupted; final snapshots and read-only resume watches preserve the observed states. The report does not claim uninterrupted watch coverage.
    • 

  • T2's original 2/3 marker result was caused by a transient konnectivity 502; the recheck via logs and direct file reads is authoritative and retained alongside the failed log.
    • 

  • The restored operator image reference is the mutable quay.io/hypershift/hypershift-operator:latest tag. The normalized Deployment manifest matches the pre-test manifest, but the restored pods resolved to a different digest set than the pre-test pods. This is a provenance limitation, not a manifest mismatch.
    • 

  • The parent DNS zone is shared with concurrent CI and is not a stable test-owned metric. Only the 12 child-zone records named for the test HostedCluster are attributed to this test.
    •
